## Narration 349

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=fawaz%3Adf57907be352%3Amalik%3A788)

<p dir="rtl"><strong>Hajj</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ خَمْسٌ مِنَ الدَّوَابِّ لَيْسَ عَلَى الْمُحْرِمِ فِي قَتْلِهِنَّ جُنَاحٌ الْغُرَابُ وَالْحِدَأَةُ وَالْعَقْرَبُ وَالْفَأْرَةُ وَالْكَلْبُ الْعَقُورُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 788]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/ara-malik/788.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Muwatta Malik — Arabic</a></p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source combines narration and commentary. Its complete text is preserved under Comments while the narration boundary is reviewed.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 788]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/eng-malik/788.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Muwatta Malik — English</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 788</strong><br>provider entry identity</p>

<p dir="auto">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API · eng-malik · entry 788</p>

<p dir="auto">Numbering: fawaz api hadithnumber</p>

<p dir="auto">Canonical mapping: not inferred</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;It is not halal to eat any game that has been hunted in the Haram, or has had a dog set after it in the Haram and then been killed outside the Haram. Anyone that does that has to pay a forfeit for what has been hunted. However, some one that sets his dog after game outside the Haram and then follows it until it is hunted down in the Haram does not have to pay any forfeit, unless he set the dog after the game near to the Haram. The game should not be eaten, however. If he set the dog loose near the Haram then he has to pay a forfeit for the game.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Allah, the Blessed and Exalted, says, &#x27;O you who trust, do not kill game while you are in ihram. Whoever of you kills game intentionally has to pay a forfeit commensurate with what he has killed in cattle which two men from among you shall judge, a sacrificial animal which reaches the Kaba, or else he makes a kaffara of either feeding poor people or the equivalent of that in fasting, so that he may taste the consequences of what he has done.&#x27; &quot; (Sura 5 ayat 95). Malik said, &quot;Someone who hunts game when he is not in ihram and then kills it while he is in ihram is in the same position as someone who buys game while he is in ihram and then kills it. Allah has forbidden killing it, and so a man who does so has to pay a forfeit for it. The position that we go by in this matter is that a forfeit is assessed for anyone who kills game while he is in ihram.&quot; Yahya said that Malik said, &quot;The best that I have heard about someone who kills game and is assessed for it is that the game which he has killed is assessed and its value in food is estimated and with that food he feeds each poor man a mudd, or fasts a day in place of each mudd. The number of poor men is considered, and if it is ten then he fasts ten days, and if it is twenty he fasts twenty days, according to how many people there are to be fed, even if there are more than sixty.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I have heard that a forfeit is assessed for someone who kills game in the Haram while he is not in ihram in the same way that it is assessed for some one who kills game in the Haram while he is in ihram .&quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;There are five kinds of animal which it is not wrong for some one in ihram to kill:crows, kites, scorpions, rats and mice, and wild dogs</p>

<p><a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/eng-malik/788.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">Source assessment — Salim al-Hilali: Sahih</p>

<p><a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/eng-malik/788.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ پانچ جانور ہیں محرم کو ان کا قتل منع نہیں ہے کوا اور چیل بچھو اور چوہا اور کاٹنے والا کتا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 788]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/urd-malik/788.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Muwatta Malik — Urdu</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="bn">
<h3>Bengali</h3>

<p dir="auto">রেওয়ায়ত ৯১. আবদুল্লাহ ইবন উমর (রাঃ) হইতে বর্ণিত, রাসূলুল্লাহ্ সাল্লাল্লাহু আলাইহি ওয়াসাল্লাম বলেন, পাঁচ প্রকার প্রাণী মুহরিম ব্যক্তি যদি বধ করে তবে তাহার কোন গুনাহ হইবে না- কাক, চিল, বিচ্ছু, ইদুর, হিংস্ৰ কুকুর (বা হিংস্র জন্তু, যথা বাঘ, চিতাবাঘ ইত্যাদি)।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 788]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/ben-malik/788.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Muwatta Malik — Bengali</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 350

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=fawaz%3Adf57907be352%3Amalik%3A789)

<p dir="rtl"><strong>Hajj</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ خَمْسٌ مِنَ الدَّوَابِّ مَنْ قَتَلَهُنَّ وَهُوَ مُحْرِمٌ فَلاَ جُنَاحَ عَلَيْهِ الْعَقْرَبُ وَالْفَأْرَةُ وَالْغُرَابُ وَالْحِدَأَةُ وَالْكَلْبُ الْعَقُورُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 789]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/ara-malik/789.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Muwatta Malik — Arabic</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Dinar from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace,said,&quot;There are five (kinds of) animal which it is not wrong for some one in ihram to kill:scorpions, rats and mice, crows, kites and wild dogs</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 789]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784/editions/eng-malik/789.json" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API — Muwatta Malik — English</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا پانچ جانور ایسے ہیں کہ جو کوئی ان کو احرام کی حالت میں مار ڈالے تو کچھ گناہ نہیں ہے ایک بچھو دوسرے چوہا تیسرے کاٹنا کتا چوتھے چیل پانچواں کوا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 789]</p>
